						AND = { exists = event_target:dtp_esl_36 is_same_species = event_target:dtp_esl_36 }
						AND = { exists = event_target:dtp_esl_37 is_same_species = event_target:dtp_esl_37 }
						AND = { exists = event_target:dtp_esl_38 is_same_species = event_target:dtp_esl_38 }
						AND = { exists = event_target:dtp_esl_39 is_same_species = event_target:dtp_esl_39 }
						AND = { exists = event_target:dtp_esl_40 is_same_species = event_target:dtp_esl_40 }
					}
				}
			}
			clear_global_event_target = dtp_tsp_16
		}
	}
	from = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_espg value = 1 }
	}
}

dtp_hn_fill = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_1
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_2
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_3
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_4
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_4
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_5
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_5
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_6
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_7
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_7
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_8
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_8
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_9
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_9
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_10
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_10
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_11
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_11
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_hn_12
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_hn_12
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_s
		}
		event_target:dtp_s = {
			set_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 0 }
			every_neighbor_system = {
				prev = {
					change_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 1 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 2 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_2
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 3 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_3
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 4 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_4
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 5 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_5
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 6 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_6
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 7 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_7
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 8 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_8
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 9 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_9
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						prev = {
							check_variable = { which = dtp_hnn value = 10 }
						}
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_hn_10
				}
				if = {

dtp_vsync = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = dtp_pe_open
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tp_1
				}
				event_target:dtp_tp_1 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_pv
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_pv
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_pv
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tp_2
				}
				event_target:dtp_tp_2 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_pv
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_pv
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_pv
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tp_3
				}
				event_target:dtp_tp_3 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_pv
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_pv
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_pv
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tp_4
				}
				event_target:dtp_tp_4 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_pv
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_pv
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_pv
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tp_5
				}
				event_target:dtp_tp_5 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_pv
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_pv
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_pv
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 6 }
			}

			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_evs
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_evs
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 16 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tc_16
				}
				event_target:dtp_tc_16 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ev
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_ev
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_ev
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_16
				}
				event_target:dtp_tsp_16 = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_evs
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tc_16
				}
				event_target:dtp_tc_16 = {
					owner_species = {
						save_global_event_target_as = dtp_evs
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_evs
				}
				clear_global_event_target = dtp_evs
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_lv
				OR = {
					NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_ev }
					event_target:dtp_lv = {
						NOT = { exists = owner }
					}
					event_target:dtp_lv = {
						owner = {
							NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:dtp_ev }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			clear_global_event_target = dtp_lv
		}
	}
}
